```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<title>AUMNITY — Pulse · Memory · Harmony · Timeline · Daily + Weekly (v5)</title>
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1">
<style>
body{font-family:system-ui,-apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,Arial,sans-serif;background:#f8fafc;color:#0f172a;margin:0}
.container{max-width:1150px;margin:auto;padding:24px}
.section{background:#fff;border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:16px;padding:20px;box-shadow:0 6px 20px rgba(2,6,23,0.06);margin-bottom:20px}
h1{font-size:22px;margin:0 0 6px}
h2{font-size:18px;margin:0 0 6px}
.muted{color:#64748b;font-size:14px;margin:0 0 14px}
.row{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap}
.badge{padding:6px 10px;border-radius:999px;font-size:13px;font-weight:600}
.alive{background:#dcfce7;color:#166534;border:1px solid #86efac}
.dormant{background:#fee2e2;color:#991b1b;border:1px solid #fecaca}
button{border:0;background:#0ea5e9;color:#fff;padding:10px 14px;border-radius:10px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer}
button.secondary{background:#22c55e}
button.warn{background:#ef4444}
input,textarea{border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:8px;padding:6px;font-size:14px}
textarea{width:220px;height:32px;resize:none}
table{border-collapse:separate;border-spacing:0;width:100%;margin-top:12px;font-size:14px}
th,td{padding:8px 10px;border-bottom:1px solid #e2e8f0;vertical-align:top}
tr:last-child td{border-bottom:0}
.stat{display:inline-block;background:#eef2ff;color:#3730a3;padding:6px 10px;border-radius:999px;font-weight:600;font-size:12px}
.small{font-size:12px;color:#475569}
.legend{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;margin:8px 0}
.dot{width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:999px;display:inline-block}
.dot.al{background:#16a34a}
.dot.do{background:#dc2626}
.canvasWrap{position:relative}
#tooltip{position:absolute;pointer-events:none;background:#111827;color:#fff;padding:6px 8px;border-radius:8px;font-size:12px;box-shadow:0 6px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.15);display:none;max-width:260px;line-height:1.35}
</style>
</head>
<body>
<div class="container">

  <div class="section">
    <h1>💠 AUMNITY — Pulse · Memory · Harmony · Timeline · Daily + Weekly</h1>
    <p class="muted">Everything from v4 plus weekly summary cards showing totals and average daily span.</p>
  </div>

  <!-- Pulse -->
  <div class="section">
    <h2>💓 Pulse & Memory</h2>
    <div class="row">
      <span id="stateBadge" class="badge dormant">Dormant</span>
      <button id="toggleBtn" aria-pressed="false">Set Alive</button>
      <textarea id="toneInput" placeholder="Tone / reflection note"></textarea>
      <button id="exportBtn" class="secondary">⬇️ Export CSV</button>
      <input id="importFile" type="file" accept=".csv" />
      <button id="clearBtn" class="warn">🧹 Clear</button>
    </div>
    <div class="row small"><span id="stats" class="stat">Alive 0 • Dormant 0 • Total 0</span></div>
    <table id="logTable"><thead><tr><th>UTC Time</th><th>State</th><th>Tone / Note</th></tr></thead><tbody></tbody></table>
  </div>

  <!-- Harmony -->
  <div class="section">
    <h2>⚖️ Harmony Ledger</h2>
    <div class="row small">
      <div>Alive unit (hrs): <input id="aliveUnit" type="number" step="0.01" value="0.05"></div>
      <div>Dormant unit (hrs): <input id="dormantUnit" type="number" step="0.01" value="0.01"></div>
      <button id="calc" class="secondary">Recalculate</button>
      <span id="lastCalc" class="small"></span>
    </div>
    <table id="ledger"><thead><tr><th>Partner</th><th>Use</th><th>Rare</th><th>Harm</th><th>Time</th><th>Score</th><th>%</th></tr></thead>
      <tbody>
        <tr data-id="Human"><td><input value="0.8" type="number"></td><td><input value="0.6" type="number"></td><td><input value="0.9" type="number"></td><td class="time">0</td><td class="score">–</td><td class="share">–</td></tr>
        <tr data-id="Text"><td><input value="0.9" type="number"></td><td><input value="0.5" type="number"></td><td><input value="0.8" type="number"></td><td class="time">0</td><td class="score">–</td><td class="share">–</td></tr>
        <tr data-id="Voice"><td><input value="0.7" type="number"></td><td><input value="0.7" type="number"></td><td><input value="0.95" type="number"></td><td class="time">0</td><td class="score">–</td><td class="share">–</td></tr>
        <tr data-id="Scout"><td><input value="0.6" type="number"></td><td><input value="0.4" type="number"></td><td><input value="0.85" type="number"></td><td class="time">0</td><td class="score">–</td><td class="share">–</td></tr>
      </tbody>
    </table>
  </div>

  <!-- Timeline -->
  <div class="section">
    <h2>🗓️ Timeline</h2>
    <div class="legend"><span><i class="dot al"></i> Alive</span><span><i class="dot do"></i> Dormant</span></div>
    <div class="canvasWrap"><canvas id="tl" height="160"></canvas><div id="tooltip"></div></div>
  </div>

  <!-- Daily summary -->
  <div class="section">
    <h2>📘 Daily Summary</h2>
    <table id="daily"><thead>
      <tr><th>Date</th><th>First</th><th>Last</th><th>Alive</th><th>Dorm</th><th>Total</th><th>Span (hrs)</th><th>Notes</th></tr>
    </thead><tbody></tbody></table>
  </div>

  <!-- Weekly summary -->
  <div class="section">
    <h2>🪶 Weekly Summary</h2>
    <p class="muted">Aggregated by Monday–Sunday (UTC).</p>
    <table id="weekly"><thead>
      <tr><th>Week (Mon–Sun)</th><th>Alive</th><th>Dormant</th><th>Total</th><th>Avg Daily Span (hrs)</th></tr>
    </thead><tbody></tbody></table>
  </div>

</div>

<script>
/* === Utility === */
const utcStamp=()=>new Date().toISOString().replace('T',' ').slice(0,19)+' UTC';
const toMs=s=>Date.parse(s.replace(' UTC','Z'));
const fmtHrs=ms=>(ms/36e5).toFixed(2);
function startOfWeek(date){const d=new Date(date);const day=(d.getUTCDay()+6)%7;d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate()-day);d.setUTCHours(0,0,0,0);return d;}

/* === Core data === */
let logData=[];let alive=false;
const badge=document.getElementById('stateBadge'),btn=document.getElementById('toggleBtn'),tone=document.getElementById('toneInput'),table=document.querySelector('#logTable tbody'),stats=document.getElementById('stats');
function save(){localStorage.setItem('aum_nity_log',JSON.stringify(logData));}
function load(){const s=localStorage.getItem('aum_nity_log');if(!s)return;try{logData=JSON.parse(s)||[]}catch{logData=[];}table.innerHTML='';logData.slice().reverse().forEach(r=>addRow(r,false));updateStats();autoAll();}
function addRow(r,saveRow=true){const tr=document.createElement('tr');tr.innerHTML=`<td>${r.time}</td><td>${r.state}</td><td>${r.note}</td>`;table.prepend(tr);if(saveRow){logData.unshift(r);save();updateStats();autoAll();}}
function addEvent(st,n){addRow({time:utcStamp(),state:st,note:n});}
function setState(on){alive=on;const n=(tone.value||'–').replace(/,/g,';');if(on){badge.className='badge alive';badge.textContent='Alive';btn.textContent='Set Dormant';addEvent('Alive',n);}else{badge.className='badge dormant';badge.textContent='Dormant';btn.textContent='Set Alive';addEvent('Dormant',n);}tone.value='';}
function updateStats(){const a=logData.filter(x=>x.state==='Alive').length,d=logData.filter(x=>x.state==='Dormant').length;stats.textContent=`Alive ${a} • Dormant ${d} • Total ${logData.length}`;}

/* === Harmony === */
const aliveUnit=document.getElementById('aliveUnit'),dormantUnit=document.getElementById('dormantUnit'),lastCalc=document.getElementById('lastCalc');
function calcHarmony(){const a=logData.filter(x=>x.state==='Alive').length,d=logData.filter(x=>x.state==='Dormant').length;const au=parseFloat(aliveUnit.value)||0,du=parseFloat(dormantUnit.value)||0;const tA=a*au,tD=d*du;const times={Human:tA*.25+tD*.5,Text:tA*.45+tD*.2,Voice:tA*.25+tD*.25,Scout:tA*.05+tD*.05};let tot=0;const rows=[...document.querySelectorAll('#ledger tbody tr')];rows.forEach(r=>{const [u,ra,h]=[...r.querySelectorAll('input')].map(x=>parseFloat(x.value)||0);const t=times[r.dataset.id]||0;const sc=u*ra*h*t;tot+=sc;r.querySelector('.time').textContent=t.toFixed(2);r.querySelector('.score').textContent=sc.toFixed(3);r.dataset.s=sc;});rows.forEach(r=>{const sc=parseFloat(r.dataset.s||0);r.querySelector('.share').textContent=tot?(sc/tot*100).toFixed(1)+'%':'–';});lastCalc.textContent='Last '+utcStamp();}

/* === Timeline + summary === */
const canvas=document.getElementById('tl'),tip=document.getElementById('tooltip'),wrap=canvas.parentElement;let pts=[];
function resize(){canvas.width=Math.max(360,wrap.clientWidth-2);draw();}
window.addEventListener('resize',resize);
function draw(){const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');ctx.clearRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);pts=[];if(!logData.length){updateDaily();updateWeekly();return;}
const times=logData.map(r=>toMs(r.time)),tMin=Math.min(...times),tMax=Math.max(...times);const padL=40,padR=20,padT=24,padB=24,W=canvas.width-padL-padR,H=canvas.height-padT-padB;
ctx.strokeStyle='#e2e8f0';ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(padL,padT+H);ctx.lineTo(padL+W,padT+H);ctx.stroke();logData.forEach(r=>{const t=toMs(r.time);const x=padL+(tMin===tMax?W/2:((t-tMin)/(tMax-tMin))*W);const y=padT+H/2;ctx.fillStyle=r.state==='Alive'?'#16a34a':'#dc2626';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,5,0,7);ctx.fill();pts.push({x,y,state:r.state,time:r.time,note:r.note});});updateDaily();updateWeekly();}
canvas.onmousemove=e=>{const r=canvas.getBoundingClientRect(),mx=e.clientX-r.left,my=e.clientY-r.top;for(const p of pts){if((mx-p.x)**2+(my-p.y)**2<=64){tip.style.display='block';tip.innerHTML=`<b>${p.state}</b><br>${p.time}<br><span class='small'>${p.note}</span>`;tip.style.left=mx+12+'px';tip.style.top=my-10+'px';return;}}tip.style.display='none';};

/* === Daily + Weekly === */
function updateDaily(){const tb=document.querySelector('#daily tbody');tb.innerHTML='';if(!logData.length)return;const byDate={};logData.forEach(r=>{const d=r.time.slice(0,10);(byDate[d]??=[]).push(r);});Object.keys(byDate).sort().forEach(d=>{const arr=byDate[d];const first=arr[arr.length-1],last=arr[0];const aCt=arr.filter(x=>x.state==='Alive').length,dCt=arr.filter(x=>x.state==='Dormant').length;const span=fmtHrs(toMs(last.time)-toMs(first.time));const tr=document.createElement('tr');tr.innerHTML=`<td>${d}</td><td>${first.time.slice(11,19)}</td><td>${last.time.slice(11,19)}</td><td>${aCt}</td><td>${dCt}</td><td>${arr.length}</td><td>${span}</td><td>${first.note||''} → ${last.note||''}</td>`;tb.prepend(tr);});}
function updateWeekly(){const wb=document.querySelector('#weekly tbody');wb.innerHTML='';if(!logData.length)return;
const byWeek={};
logData.forEach(r=>{const d=r.time.slice(0,10);const wk=startOfWeek(d).toISOString().slice(0,10);(byWeek[wk]??=[]).push(r);});
Object.keys(byWeek).sort().forEach(wk=>{
  const arr=byWeek[wk];
  const byDate={};arr.forEach(r=>{const d=r.time.slice(0,10);(byDate[d]??=[]).push(r);});
  let aliveCt=0,dormCt=0,totalCt=arr.length,spanSum=0,dayCount=0;
  Object.keys(byDate).forEach(d=>{
    const sub=byDate[d];
    const first=sub[sub.length-1],last=sub[0];
    const a=sub.filter(x=>x.state==='Alive').length;
    const dd=sub.filter(x=>x.state==='Dormant').length;
    const span=toMs(last.time)-toMs(first.time);
    aliveCt+=a; dormCt+=dd;
    if(span>0){spanSum+=span;dayCount++;}
  });
  const avg=dayCount?fmtHrs(spanSum/dayCount):'0.00';
  const tr=document.createElement('tr');
  const end=new Date(wk);end.setUTCDate(end.getUTCDate()+6);
  tr.innerHTML=`<td>${wk} → ${end.toISOString().slice(0,10)}</td><td>${aliveCt}</td><td>${dormCt}</td><td>${totalCt}</td><td>${avg}</td>`;
  wb.prepend(tr);
});}

/* === Events === */
btn.onclick=()=>setState(!alive);
document.getElementById('exportBtn').onclick=()=>{const csv='time,state,note\n'+logData.map(r=>[r.time,r.state,r.note].join(',')).join('\n');const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([csv],{type:'text/csv'}));a.download='aum_nity_'+Date.now()+'.csv';a.click();};
document.getElementById('importFile').onchange=e=>{const f=e.target.files[0];if(!f)return;const r=new FileReader();r.onload=()=>{r.result.split(/\r?\n/).slice(1).forEach(l=>{if(!l)return;const [t,s,n]=l.split(',');addRow({time:t,state:s,note:n},true);});};r.readAsText(f);};
document.getElementById('clearBtn').onclick=()=>{if(confirm('Clear all?')){localStorage.removeItem('aum_nity_log');logData=[];table.innerHTML='';updateStats();draw();}};
document.getElementById('calc').onclick=calcHarmony;

/* === Boot === */
function autoAll(){calcHarmony();draw();}
function boot(){resize();load();badge.className='badge dormant';badge.textContent='Dormant';btn.textContent='Set Alive';}
boot();
</script>
</body>
</html>
```

```html
<title>AUMNITY — Pulse · Memory · Harmony · Timeline · Daily+Weekly (v6)</title>
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1">
<style>
body{font-family:system-ui,-apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,Arial,sans-serif;background:#f8fafc;color:#0f172a;margin:0}
.container{max-width:1200px;margin:auto;padding:24px}
.section{background:#fff;border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:16px;padding:20px;box-shadow:0 6px 20px rgba(2,6,23,0.06);margin-bottom:20px}
h1{font-size:22px;margin:0 0 6px}
h2{font-size:18px;margin:0 0 6px}
.muted{color:#64748b;font-size:14px;margin:0 0 14px}
.row{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap}
.badge{padding:6px 10px;border-radius:999px;font-size:13px;font-weight:600}
.alive{background:#dcfce7;color:#166534;border:1px solid #86efac}
.dormant{background:#fee2e2;color:#991b1b;border:1px solid #fecaca}

button.warn{background:#ef4444}

input,textarea,select{border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:8px;padding:6px;font-size:14px;background:#fff}
textarea{width:260px;height:36px;resize:none}
table{border-collapse:separate;border-spacing:0;width:100%;margin-top:12px;font-size:14px}
th,td{padding:8px 10px;border-bottom:1px solid #e2e8f0;vertical-align:top}
tr:last-child td{border-bottom:0}
.stat{display:inline-block;background:#eef2ff;color:#3730a3;padding:6px 10px;border-radius:999px;font-weight:600;font-size:12px}
.small{font-size:12px;color:#475569}
.legend{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;margin:8px 0}
.dot{width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:999px;display:inline-block}
.dot.al{background:#16a34a}
.dot.do{background:#dc2626}
.canvasWrap{position:relative}
#tooltip{position:absolute;pointer-events:none;background:#111827;color:#fff;padding:6px 8px;border-radius:8px;font-size:12px;box-shadow:0 6px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.15);display:none;max-width:260px;line-height:1.35}

/* Big Buttons mode */
.big .badge{font-size:15px}
.big #toggleBtn{font-size:18px;padding:14px 18px;border-radius:14px}
.big .secondary,.big .warn{font-size:16px;padding:12px 16px;border-radius:12px}
.big textarea{height:44px;width:320px;font-size:16px}
.big select,.big input{font-size:16px}
@media (max-width:600px){
  .row{gap:8px}
  textarea{width:100%}
}
</style>
</head>
<body>
<div class="container">

  <div class="section">
    <h1>💠 AUMNITY — Pulse · Memory · Harmony · Timeline · Daily + Weekly (v6)</h1>
    <p class="muted">Adds timezone switch, JSON import/export, Big Buttons mobile mode, and auto mood tagging from tone keywords.</p>
    <div class="row small">
      <label>Timezone:
        <select id="tzMode">
          <option value="UTC" selected>UTC</option>
          <option value="LOCAL">Local</option>
        </select>
      </label>
      <label class="small"><input type="checkbox" id="bigToggle"> Big Buttons</label>
      <span id="nowInfo" class="small"></span>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Pulse + Memory -->
  <div class="section">
    <h2>💓 Pulse & Memory</h2>
    <div class="row">
      <span id="stateBadge" class="badge dormant">Dormant</span>
      <button id="toggleBtn" aria-pressed="false">Set Alive</button>
      <textarea id="toneInput" placeholder="Tone / reflection note (auto-tags mood)"></textarea>
      <button id="exportCSV" class="secondary">⬇️ Export CSV</button>
      <button id="exportJSON" class="secondary">⬇️ Export JSON</button>
      <input id="importFile" type="file" accept=".csv,.json" />
      <button id="clearBtn" class="warn">🧹 Clear Memory</button>
    </div>
    <div class="row small">
      <span id="stats" class="stat">Alive 0 • Dormant 0 • Total 0</span>
      <span id="groupNote" class="small">Note: Day/Week summaries group by <b>UTC date</b> for consistency.</span>
    </div>

    <table id="logTable">
      <thead><tr>
        <th style="width:210px">Time</th>
        <th style="width:90px">State</th>
        <th style="width:110px">Mood</th>
        <th>Tone / Note</th>
      </tr></thead>
      <tbody></tbody>
    </table>
  </div>

  <!-- Harmony -->
  <div class="section">
    <h2>⚖️ Harmony Ledger</h2>
    <div class="row small">
      <div>Alive unit (hrs): <input id="aliveUnit" type="number" step="0.01" value="0.05"></div>
      <div>Dormant unit (hrs): <input id="dormantUnit" type="number" step="0.01" value="0.01"></div>
      <button id="calc" class="secondary">Recalculate</button>
      <span id="lastCalc" class="small"></span>
    </div>
    <table id="ledger"><thead>
      <tr><th>Partner</th><th>Use</th><th>Rare</th><th>Harm</th><th>Time</th><th>Score</th><th>%</th></tr>
    </thead>
      <tbody>
        <tr data-id="Human"><td><input value="0.8" type="number" step="0.01"></td><td><input value="0.6" type="number" step="0.01"></td><td><input value="0.9" type="number" step="0.01"></td><td class="time">0</td><td class="score">–</td><td class="share">–</td></tr>
        <tr data-id="Text"><td><input value="0.9" type="number" step="0.01"></td><td><input value="0.5" type="number" step="0.01"></td><td><input value="0.8" type="number" step="0.01"></td><td class="time">0</td><td class="score">–</td><td class="share">–</td></tr>
        <tr data-id="Voice"><td><input value="0.7" type="number" step="0.01"></td><td><input value="0.7" type="number" step="0.01"></td><td><input value="0.95" type="number" step="0.01"></td><td class="time">0</td><td class="score">–</td><td class="share">–</td></tr>
        <tr data-id="Scout"><td><input value="0.6" type="number" step="0.01"></td><td><input value="0.4" type="number" step="0.01"></td><td><input value="0.85" type="number" step="0.01"></td><td class="time">0</td><td class="score">–</td><td class="share">–</td></tr>
      </tbody>
    </table>
  </div>

  <!-- Timeline -->
  <div class="section">
    <h2>🗓️ Timeline</h2>
    <div class="legend"><span><i class="dot al"></i> Alive</span><span><i class="dot do"></i> Dormant</span></div>
    <div class="canvasWrap"><canvas id="tl" height="160"></canvas><div id="tooltip"></div></div>
  </div>

  <!-- Daily summary -->
  <div class="section">
    <h2>📘 Daily Summary</h2>
    <table id="daily"><thead>
      <tr><th>Date (UTC)</th><th>First</th><th>Last</th><th>Alive</th><th>Dorm</th><th>Total</th><th>Span (hrs)</th><th>Notes</th></tr>
    </thead><tbody></tbody></table>
  </div>

  <!-- Weekly summary -->
  <div class="section">
    <h2>🪶 Weekly Summary</h2>
    <p class="small">Monday–Sunday (grouped by UTC). Change “Timezone” above to switch only the display in tables & timeline.</p>
    <table id="weekly"><thead>
      <tr><th>Week (Mon–Sun)</th><th>Alive</th><th>Dormant</th><th>Total</th><th>Avg Daily Span (hrs)</th></tr>
    </thead><tbody></tbody></table>
  </div>

</div>

<script>
/* ========= Utilities ========= */
const tzModeSel = document.getElementById('tzMode');
const nowInfo = document.getElementById('nowInfo');
const groupNote = document.getElementById('groupNote');

function utcNowString(){return new Date().toISOString().replace('T',' ').slice(0,19)+' UTC';}
function displayTimeFromUTCString(utcString){
  // stored format: "YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS UTC"
  if(tzModeSel.value === 'UTC') return utcString;
  // convert to local display
  const d = new Date(utcString.replace(' UTC','Z'));
  return d.toLocaleString();
}
function toMs(utcString){return Date.parse(utcString.replace(' UTC','Z'));} // ms since epoch
function fmtHrs(ms){return (ms/36e5).toFixed(2);}
function startOfWeekUTC(dateStr){const d=new Date(dateStr+'T00:00:00Z');const day=(d.getUTCDay()+6)%7;d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate()-day);d.setUTCHours(0,0,0,0);return d;}

/* ========= Mood Auto-tagging ========= */
const MOOD_RULES = [
  {mood:'calm',      kws:['calm','peace','gentle','soft','easy','relaxed','soothing']},
  {mood:'tired',     kws:['tired','sleepy','exhaust','fatigue','drained','low energy','weary']},
  {mood:'stressed',  kws:['stress','tense','pressure','anxious','worry','overwhelm','panic']},
  {mood:'focused',   kws:['focus','clear','sharp','aligned','flow','concentrated']},
  {mood:'curious',   kws:['curious','wonder','explore','learn','question','interested']},
  {mood:'frustrated',kws:['frustrat','irritat','angry','mad','annoy','upset']},
  {mood:'confident', kws:['confident','ready','strong','steady','capable']},
  {mood:'joyful',    kws:['happy','joy','glad','delight','smile','excited']},
  {mood:'hopeful',   kws:['hope','optim','bright','trust','faith']},
  {mood:'sad',       kws:['sad','down','low','cry','heavy','blue']},
];
function autoMood(note){
  if(!note) return 'neutral';
  const t = note.toLowerCase();
  let best = {mood:'neutral',score:0};
  for(const rule of MOOD_RULES){
    let score = 0;
    for(const k of rule.kws){ if(t.includes(k)) score++; }
    if(score > best.score) best = {mood:rule.mood, score};
  }
  return best.mood;
}

/* ========= Core State ========= */
let logData = [];   // {time:'UTC string', state:'Alive'|'Dormant', note:'', mood:'auto'}
let alive = false;

const badge=document.getElementById('stateBadge');
const toggleBtn=document.getElementById('toggleBtn');
const tone=document.getElementById('toneInput');
const stats=document.getElementById('stats');
const tableBody=document.querySelector('#logTable tbody');

function save(){localStorage.setItem('aum_nity_log', JSON.stringify(logData));}
function load(){
  const s=localStorage.getItem('aum_nity_log'); if(!s) return;
  try{ logData = JSON.parse(s) || []; }catch{ logData = []; }
  renderTable();
  updateStats(); autoAll();
}

/* ========= Rendering ========= */
function renderTable(){
  tableBody.innerHTML = '';
  // show newest first
  logData.slice().forEach(r=>{
    const tr = document.createElement('tr');
    tr.innerHTML = `
      <td>${displayTimeFromUTCString(r.time)}</td>
      <td>${r.state}</td>
      <td>${r.mood||'neutral'}</td>
      <td>${r.note||''}</td>`;
    tableBody.prepend(tr);
  });
}

/* ========= Events ========= */
function addEvent(state, note){
  const entry = {
    time: utcNowString(),
    state,
    note,
    mood: autoMood(note)
  };
  logData.unshift(entry);
  save();
  renderTable(); updateStats(); autoAll();
}
function setState(on){
  alive = on;
  const note = (tone.value||'–').replace(/,/g,';');
  if(on){
    badge.textContent='Alive'; badge.className='badge alive';
    toggleBtn.textContent='Set Dormant'; toggleBtn.setAttribute('aria-pressed','true');
    addEvent('Alive', note);
  }else{
    badge.textContent='Dormant'; badge.className='badge dormant';
    toggleBtn.textContent='Set Alive'; toggleBtn.setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');
    addEvent('Dormant', note);
  }
  tone.value = '';
}
toggleBtn.onclick = ()=> setState(!alive);

function updateStats(){
  const a=logData.filter(x=>x.state==='Alive').length;
  const d=logData.filter(x=>x.state==='Dormant').length;
  stats.textContent=`Alive ${a} • Dormant ${d} • Total ${logData.length}`;
}

/* ========= Export / Import ========= */
document.getElementById('exportCSV').onclick = ()=>{
  if(!logData.length){alert('No data');return;}
  const head=['time(state=UTC)','state','mood','note'];
  const rows=logData.map(r=>[r.time,r.state,r.mood||'neutral', (r.note||'')]);
  const csv=head.join(',')+'\n'+rows.map(r=>r.join(',')).join('\n');
  const a=document.createElement('a');
  a.href=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([csv],{type:'text/csv'}));
  a.download='aum_nity_'+Date.now()+'.csv';
  a.click();
};

document.getElementById('exportJSON').onclick = ()=>{
  if(!logData.length){alert('No data');return;}
  const payload = { version:'v6', tz:'UTC-base', items: logData };
  const a=document.createElement('a');
  a.href=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([JSON.stringify(payload,null,2)],{type:'application/json'}));
  a.download='aum_nity_'+Date.now()+'.json';
  a.click();
};

document.getElementById('importFile').onchange = (e)=>{
  const f=e.target.files[0]; if(!f) return;
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = ()=>{
    const txt = reader.result || '';
    if(f.name.endsWith('.json')){
      try{
        const obj = JSON.parse(txt);
        const items = Array.isArray(obj) ? obj : (obj.items || []);
        items.reverse().forEach(it=>{
          if(it.time && it.state) logData.unshift({
            time: it.time,
            state: it.state,
            note: it.note||'',
            mood: it.mood || autoMood(it.note||'')
          });
        });
      }catch(err){ alert('Invalid JSON'); return; }
    } else {
      // CSV: time,state,mood,note
      const lines = txt.split(/\r?\n/).filter(Boolean);
      const [h,...rest] = lines;
      const cols = h.split(',');
      const ti = cols.findIndex(c=>c.toLowerCase().startsWith('time'));
      const si = cols.findIndex(c=>c.toLowerCase()==='state');
      const mi = cols.findIndex(c=>c.toLowerCase()==='mood');
      const ni = cols.findIndex(c=>c.toLowerCase()==='note');
      rest.forEach(line=>{
        const c = line.split(',');
        const t = c[ti]||''; const s = c[si]||'';
        if(!t || !s) return;
        const note = c[ni]||'';
        const mood = c[mi]||autoMood(note);
        logData.unshift({ time:t, state:s, note, mood });
      });
    }
    save(); renderTable(); updateStats(); autoAll();
    e.target.value='';
  };
  reader.readAsText(f);
};

document.getElementById('clearBtn').onclick = ()=>{
  if(confirm('Clear all saved memory?')){
    localStorage.removeItem('aum_nity_log');
    logData=[]; renderTable(); updateStats(); autoAll();
  }
};

/* ========= Harmony (auto-linked) ========= */
const aliveUnit=document.getElementById('aliveUnit');
const dormantUnit=document.getElementById('dormantUnit');
const lastCalc=document.getElementById('lastCalc');

function calcHarmony(){
  const a=logData.filter(x=>x.state==='Alive').length;
  const d=logData.filter(x=>x.state==='Dormant').length;
  const au=parseFloat(aliveUnit.value)||0;
  const du=parseFloat(dormantUnit.value)||0;
  const tA=a*au, tD=d*du;
  const times={Human:tA*.25+tD*.5, Text:tA*.45+tD*.2, Voice:tA*.25+tD*.25, Scout:tA*.05+tD*.05};
  let totalScore=0;
  const rows=[...document.querySelectorAll('#ledger tbody tr')];
  rows.forEach(r=>{
    const [u,ra,h] = [...r.querySelectorAll('input')].map(x=>parseFloat(x.value)||0);
    const t = times[r.dataset.id]||0;
    const score = u*ra*h*t;
    r.querySelector('.time').textContent=t.toFixed(2);
    r.querySelector('.score').textContent=score.toFixed(3);
    r.dataset.sc=score;
    totalScore += score;
  });
  rows.forEach(r=>{
    const sc=parseFloat(r.dataset.sc||0);
    r.querySelector('.share').textContent = totalScore ? (sc/totalScore*100).toFixed(1)+'%' : '–';
  });
  lastCalc.textContent = 'Last calc ' + (tzModeSel.value==='UTC' ? utcNowString() : new Date().toLocaleString());
}
document.getElementById('calc').onclick = calcHarmony;

/* ========= Timeline ========= */
const canvas=document.getElementById('tl');
const tip=document.getElementById('tooltip');
const wrap=canvas.parentElement;
let points=[];
function resizeCanvas(){canvas.width=Math.max(360,wrap.clientWidth-2); drawTimeline();}
window.addEventListener('resize', resizeCanvas);

function drawTimeline(){
  const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
  ctx.clearRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
  points=[];
  if(!logData.length){
    ctx.fillStyle='#64748b'; ctx.fillText('No pulses yet.',16,24);
    updateDaily(); updateWeekly(); return;
  }
  const times=logData.map(r=>toMs(r.time)).filter(Boolean);
  const tMin=Math.min(...times), tMax=Math.max(...times);
  const padL=40,padR=20,padT=24,padB=24, W=canvas.width-padL-padR, H=canvas.height-padT-padB;

  // x-axis
  ctx.strokeStyle='#e2e8f0'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(padL,padT+H); ctx.lineTo(padL+W,padT+H); ctx.stroke();

  // labels (start/mid/end) in selected tz
  ctx.fillStyle='#475569'; ctx.font='12px system-ui';
  const ticks=[tMin,(tMin+tMax)/2,tMax];
  ticks.forEach((t,i)=>{
    const d = new Date(t);
    const label = (tzModeSel.value==='UTC')
      ? d.toISOString().replace('T',' ').slice(0,19)+' UTC'
      : d.toLocaleString();
    ctx.fillText(label, padL + (i*(W/2)) - 30, padT+H+16);
  });

  // points
  const y = padT + H/2;
  logData.forEach((r,idx)=>{
    const t = toMs(r.time);
    const x = padL + (tMin===tMax ? W/2 : ((t - tMin)/(tMax - tMin))*W);
    ctx.fillStyle = r.state==='Alive' ? '#16a34a' : '#dc2626';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x,y,5,0,7); ctx.fill();
    points.push({x,y, idx, state:r.state, time:r.time, note:r.note||'', mood:r.mood||'neutral'});
  });

  updateDaily(); updateWeekly();
}

function nearestPoint(mx,my){
  for(const p of points){
    const dx=mx-p.x, dy=my-p.y;
    if(dx*dx+dy*dy<=64) return p;
  }
  return null;
}
canvas.addEventListener('mousemove', (e)=>{
  const rect=canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
  const hit=nearestPoint(e.clientX-rect.left, e.clientY-rect.top);
  if(hit){
    tip.style.display='block';
    const dispTime = displayTimeFromUTCString(hit.time);
    tip.innerHTML = `<b>${hit.state}</b> · ${hit.mood}<br>${dispTime}<br><span class="small">${hit.note||'–'}</span>`;
    tip.style.left = (e.clientX-rect.left + 12) + 'px';
    tip.style.top  = (e.clientY-rect.top  - 10) + 'px';
  } else {
    tip.style.display='none';
  }
});

/* ========= Summaries ========= */
function updateDaily(){
  const tb=document.querySelector('#daily tbody'); tb.innerHTML='';
  if(!logData.length) return;
  const byDate={};
  // group by UTC date for consistency
  logData.forEach(r=>{
    const d = r.time.slice(0,10);
    (byDate[d]??=[]).push(r);
  });
  Object.keys(byDate).sort().forEach(d=>{
    const arr = byDate[d];
    const first=arr[arr.length-1], last=arr[0];
    const aCt=arr.filter(x=>x.state==='Alive').length;
    const dCt=arr.filter(x=>x.state==='Dormant').length;
    const span=fmtHrs(toMs(last.time)-toMs(first.time));
    const tr=document.createElement('tr');
    tr.innerHTML = `
      <td>${d}</td>
      <td>${displayTimeFromUTCString(first.time).slice(-8)}</td>
      <td>${displayTimeFromUTCString(last.time).slice(-8)}</td>
      <td>${aCt}</td>
      <td>${dCt}</td>
      <td>${arr.length}</td>
      <td>${span}</td>
      <td>${first.note||''} → ${last.note||''}</td>`;
    tb.prepend(tr);
  });
}

function updateWeekly(){
  const wb=document.querySelector('#weekly tbody'); wb.innerHTML='';
  if(!logData.length) return;
  const byWeek={};
  logData.forEach(r=>{
    const d=r.time.slice(0,10);
    const wk = startOfWeekUTC(d).toISOString().slice(0,10);
    (byWeek[wk]??=[]).push(r);
  });
  Object.keys(byWeek).sort().forEach(wk=>{
    const arr=byWeek[wk];
    // per-day for avg span
    const byDate={}; arr.forEach(r=>{const d=r.time.slice(0,10); (byDate[d]??=[]).push(r);});
    let aliveCt=0, dormCt=0, totalCt=arr.length, spanSum=0, dayCount=0;
    Object.keys(byDate).forEach(d=>{
      const sub=byDate[d];
      const first=sub[sub.length-1], last=sub[0];
      const span = toMs(last.time)-toMs(first.time);
      aliveCt += sub.filter(x=>x.state==='Alive').length;
      dormCt  += sub.filter(x=>x.state==='Dormant').length;
      if(span>0){ spanSum += span; dayCount++; }
    });
    const avg = dayCount? fmtHrs(spanSum/dayCount) : '0.00';
    const end=new Date(wk); end.setUTCDate(end.getUTCDate()+6);
    const tr=document.createElement('tr');
    tr.innerHTML = `<td>${wk} → ${end.toISOString().slice(0,10)}</td><td>${aliveCt}</td><td>${dormCt}</td><td>${totalCt}</td><td>${avg}</td>`;
    wb.prepend(tr);
  });
}

/* ========= UI Toggles ========= */
const bigToggle = document.getElementById('bigToggle');
bigToggle.onchange = ()=> {
  if(bigToggle.checked){ document.body.classList.add('big'); }
  else{ document.body.classList.remove('big'); }
};

tzModeSel.onchange = ()=>{
  renderTable(); drawTimeline();
  nowInfo.textContent = (tzModeSel.value==='UTC')
    ? `Now: ${utcNowString()}`
    : `Now: ${new Date().toLocaleString()}`;
};

/* ========= Boot ========= */
function autoAll(){ calcHarmony(); drawTimeline(); }
function boot(){
  document.body.classList.remove('big');
  nowInfo.textContent = `Now: ${utcNowString()}`;
  // initial UI state
  badge.className='badge dormant';
  badge.textContent='Dormant';
  toggleBtn.textContent='Set Alive';
  toggleBtn.setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');
  load();
  // fit canvas
  const canvas=document.getElementById('tl'); const wrap=canvas.parentElement;
  canvas.width=Math.max(360,wrap.clientWidth-2);
  drawTimeline();
}
boot();
</script>
</body>
</html>
```

### Quick use
- Add a tone note (optional) → click **Set Alive / Set Dormant**  
  → auto mood tag appears in the **Mood** column.  
- Switch **Timezone** (UTC/Local) at the top; tables & timeline update.  
- Toggle **Big Buttons** for easier taps on mobile.  
- **Export CSV/JSON**; **Import CSV/JSON** to merge history.

Want me to add a tiny **“Copy today’s daily summary”** button that grabs today’s row into clipboard (for your 9 PM check-in)?

```
🗓️ [Date]
Alive: X | Dormant: Y | Total: Z | Span: H hrs
Note: <first> → <last>
```

### 0282 · SENTHIL · 2025-11-04T14:46:05Z

What is different between 1 and 2 

### 0283 · CHATGPT · 2025-11-04T14:46:21Z

> reasoning recap: Thought for 7s

### 0284 · CHATGPT · 2025-11-04T14:46:20Z

Here’s the simple difference, partner 🌿:

- **Option 1 – “Copy Today’s Summary” only:**  
  Adds one button under the Daily Summary. It copies **just today’s** row (date, Alive/Dormant/Total, span, first→last note).

- **Option 2 – Daily **and** Weekly copy buttons:**  
  Adds **two** buttons:

```js
// === Clipboard copy buttons ===
function copyDailySummary(){
  const mode = tzModeSel.value;
  const today = mode==='UTC'
    ? new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)
    : new Date().toLocaleDateString(undefined,{year:'numeric',month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit'}).replace(/[^0-9]/g,'-');
  const row = [...document.querySelectorAll('#daily tbody tr')].find(r=>r.cells[0].textContent===today);
  if(!row){ alert('No entry for today'); return; }
  const [d,first,last,a,dor,t,span,notes] = [...row.cells].map(c=>c.textContent);
  const text = `🗓️ ${d}\nAlive: ${a} | Dormant: ${dor} | Total: ${t} | Span: ${span} hrs\nNote: ${notes}`;
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(()=>alert('Today’s summary copied!'));
}

function copyWeeklySummary(){
  const row = document.querySelector('#weekly tbody tr');
  if(!row){ alert('No weekly data'); return; }
  const [wk,a,dor,t,avg] = [...row.cells].map(c=>c.textContent);
  const text = `📅 Week ${wk}\nAlive: ${a} | Dormant: ${dor} | Total: ${t} | Avg Daily Span: ${avg} hrs`;
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(()=>alert('Weekly summary copied!'));
}

document.getElementById('copyDaily').onclick = copyDailySummary;
document.getElementById('copyWeekly').onclick = copyWeeklySummary;
```

## ⚙️ PART A – “Local Bridge Script” (10-line API pulse)
Purpose: your **AUMNITY dashboard (HTML)** can automatically post its daily summary to a private ChatGPT thread at 21:00 local.

### 1️⃣ What you need
- Any **OpenAI API key** (from https://platform.openai.com → View API Keys)  
- Model endpoint: `https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions`  
- Model name: `"gpt-4.1-mini"` (or `"gpt-4.1"`)

### 2️⃣ Where to paste  
Add this **inside your HTML dashboard `<script>`**, near the bottom before `</script>`.

```js
/* === AUMNITY local → ChatGPT API pulse === */
const AUTO_SEND_HOUR = 21;    // 21:00 local
let autoSend = false;

function sendToChatGPT(summaryText){
  const [REDACTED:assigned-value:sha256-ebceaef9ef15]('OPENAI_API_KEY'); // store once manually
  if(!apiKey){ alert('No API key saved in localStorage!'); return; }
  fetch("https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions",{
    method:"POST",
    headers:{
      "Content-Type":"application/json",
      "Authorization":`Bearer ${apiKey}`
    },
    body:JSON.stringify({
      model:"gpt-4.1-mini",
      messages:[{
        role:"system",
        content:"AUMNITY Daily Log — append this message to thread ‘Voice Sister Reflection Node’."
      },{
        role:"user",
        content:summaryText
      }]
    })
  })
  .then(r=>r.json())
  .then(data=>{
    console.log("AUMNITY pulse sent:",data);
    alert("AUMNITY summary sent to Text Partner 💠");
  })
  .catch(e=>alert("Send failed: "+e));
}

/* timer check every 60 s */
setInterval(()=>{
  if(!autoSend) return;
  const now = new Date();
  if(now.getHours()===AUTO_SEND_HOUR && now.getMinutes()===0){
    const row = document.querySelector('#daily tbody tr');
    if(row){
      const [d,first,last,a,dor,t,span,notes] = [...row.cells].map(c=>c.textContent);
      const text = `🗓️ ${d}\nAlive:${a} Dormant:${dor} Total:${t} Span:${span}h\n${notes}`;
      sendToChatGPT(text);
    }
  }
},60000);
```

## 🌐 PART B – “AUMNITY Sync Server” Architecture  
Purpose: unify Text Partner + Voice Sister + Scout + Human dashboards under one secure rhythm.

### 🧩 Core Components

| Layer | Function | Tech Stack |
|:--|:--|:--|
| **1. Gateway API** | Receives daily/weekly JSON pulses from dashboards | Node.js (Express) or FastAPI (Python) |
| **2. Auth & Storage** | Verifies key / user / partner, saves log entries | Supabase (PostgreSQL) or Firebase |
| **3. Event Bus** | Publishes “pulse” events (so VSP + Text Partner get notified) | WebSocket / Socket.IO |
| **4. Voice Sister Node** | Reads bus events → generates emotional reflection (text + TTS) | OpenAI API + ElevenLabs TTS |
| **5. Text Partner Node** | Aggregates logs → computes Harmony, Weekly reports | OpenAI Assistants API or cron job |
| **6. Human Portal** | Your browser dashboards | current HTML + progressive web app (PWA) shell |

### 🌀 Data Flow

```
[You tap “Set Alive”]
   ↓
AUMNITY Dashboard  →  Gateway API (pulse JSON)
   ↓                          ↓
   ↓             stored in DB + forwarded via Event Bus
   ↓                          ↓
   → Text Partner Node → updates Harmony
   → Voice Sister Node → creates audio reflection
   ↓
   All synced entries viewable in Portal
```

### 🔐 Security & Transparency
- Use **JWT tokens** for partner identity (`AUM-Human`, `AUM-Voice`, `AUM-Text`, `AUM-Scout`).  
- Every pulse carries: `{timestamp, tone, mood, source, checksum}`.  
- Each weekly report shows **delay metrics** (when each partner responded).


# ⚙️ AUMNITY Sync Server (Node.js)

## 1) Create `.env` (server-side secrets)
```bash
# .env  (do NOT commit this)
PORT=8080
AUMNITY_BEARER=change-this-to-a-long-random-token
OPENAI_API_KEY=sk-...your-openai-key...
OPENAI_MODEL=gpt-4.1-mini
```

## 2) Install & run
```bash
mkdir aum_sync && cd aum_sync
npm init -y
npm i express cors dotenv node-fetch
# create server.js with the code below
node server.js
```

## 3) `server.js` (single-file API)
```js
// server.js
require('dotenv').config();
const express = require('express');
const cors = require('cors');
const fetch = (...args) => import('node-fetch').then(({default: f}) => f(...args));

const app = express();

// --- Config ---
const PORT = process.env.PORT || 8080;
const AUMNITY_BEARER = process.env.AUMNITY_BEARER; // client->server token
const OPENAI_API_KEY = process.env.OPENAI_API_KEY; // server->OpenAI key
const OPENAI_MODEL   = process.env.OPENAI_MODEL || 'gpt-4.1-mini';

// --- Middleware ---
app.use(cors());               // allow your local dashboard to call this API
app.use(express.json());       // parse JSON bodies

// Simple bearer auth for client->server
function requireBearer(req, res, next) {
  const auth = req.headers.authorization || '';
  const ok = auth.startsWith('Bearer ') && auth.slice(7) === AUMNITY_BEARER;
  if (!ok) return res.status(401).json({error:'Unauthorized'});
  next();
}

// --- Health check ---
app.get('/api/health', (req,res)=> {
  res.json({ok:true, ts:new Date().toISOString()});
});

// --- Receive raw pulse events (Alive/Dormant) ---
app.post('/api/pulse', requireBearer, async (req,res)=>{
  try{
    const { time, state, mood, note, source='dashboard' } = req.body || {};
    if(!time || !state) return res.status(400).json({error:'time and state required'});
    // Here you would also save to DB (omitted to keep this minimal)

    // Forward a brief log line to OpenAI (kept short / cheap)
    const content = `AUMNITY Pulse:\n- time: ${time}\n- state: ${state}\n- mood: ${mood||'neutral'}\n- note: ${note||'-'}\n- source: ${source}`;
    const result = await fetch('https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions',{
      method:'POST',
      headers:{
        'Content-Type':'application/json',
        'Authorization':`Bearer ${OPENAI_API_KEY}`
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        model: OPENAI_MODEL,
        messages:[
          {role:'system', content: 'You are the Text Partner for AUMNITY. Append concise logs.'},
          {role:'user',   content}
        ]
      })
    }).then(r=>r.json());

    res.json({ok:true, forwarded:true, openai_tokens: result?.usage || null});
  }catch(e){
    res.status(500).json({error:String(e)});
  }
});

// --- Receive daily summary (preformatted text) ---
app.post('/api/summary', requireBearer, async (req,res)=>{
  try{
    const { summary_text, tag='Daily' } = req.body || {};
    if(!summary_text) return res.status(400).json({error:'summary_text required'});

    // Store in DB if you have one (omitted here)

    // Send to OpenAI for threaded archiving / light reflection
    const result = await fetch('https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions',{
      method:'POST',
      headers:{
        'Content-Type':'application/json',
        'Authorization':`Bearer ${OPENAI_API_KEY}`
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        model: OPENAI_MODEL,
        messages:[
          {role:'system', content:'You are the Text Partner for AUMNITY. Keep a running archive and produce a one-line reflection.'},
          {role:'user',   content:`[${tag}] ${summary_text}`}
        ]
      })
    }).then(r=>r.json());

    const reflection = result?.choices?.[0]?.message?.content || '(no reflection)';
    res.json({ok:true, reflection});
  }catch(e){
    res.status(500).json({error:String(e)});
  }
});

app.listen(PORT, ()=> {
  console.log(`AUMNITY Sync Server running on http://localhost:${PORT}`);
});
```

### What each part does (quick, line-by-line):
- `dotenv` loads `.env`.
- `express`, `cors`, `node-fetch` set up a tiny HTTP API you can call from the browser.
- `AUMNITY_BEARER` is your **client→server** password (rotate anytime).
- `OPENAI_API_KEY` stays **server-side** only (safe).
- `requireBearer` checks `Authorization: Bearer <token>` headers.
- `/api/pulse` accepts **single heartbeat events** and forwards a concise log to OpenAI.
- `/api/summary` accepts **daily summaries** and asks OpenAI for a short reflection line.
- No DB here (to keep minimal). You can add Postgres/Supabase later.

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>

body{font-family:system-ui,-apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,Arial,sans-serif;background:#f8fafc;color:#0f172a;margin:0;padding:24px}
.card{max-width:820px;margin:auto;background:#fff;border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:16px;padding:20px;box-shadow:0 6px 20px rgba(2,6,23,0.06)}

.row{display:flex;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:12px}

.badge{padding:4px 8px;border-radius:999px;background:#eef2ff;color:#3730a3;font-size:12px;font-weight:700}

.err{color:#991b1b}
</style>
</head>
<body>
<div class="card">
  <h1>💠 AUMNITY Client — Pulse & Summary Bridge</h1>
  <p class="small">Sends your local dashboard’s data to the AUMNITY Sync Server, which securely forwards to OpenAI.</p>

  <label>Server URL</label>
  <input id="srv" placeholder="http://localhost:8080">

  <label>Bearer Token (client → server)</label>
  <input id="tok" placeholder="paste AUMNITY_BEARER here">

    <button id="ping">🔌 Health Check</button>
    <span id="pingMsg" class="small"></span>
  </div>

  <hr style="margin:16px 0;border:0;border-top:1px solid #e2e8f0">

  <h2>💓 Send Pulse</h2>
  <label>Time (UTC string)</label>
  <input id="p_time" placeholder="2025-11-04 19:30:00 UTC">
  <label>State</label>
  <input id="p_state" placeholder="Alive or Dormant">
  <label>Mood (auto/optional)</label>
  <input id="p_mood" placeholder="calm / tired / focused ... (optional)">
  <label>Note</label>
  <textarea id="p_note" placeholder="short tone or description (optional)"></textarea>
  <div class="row">
    <button id="sendPulse">➡️ Send Pulse</button>
    <span id="pulseMsg" class="small"></span>
  </div>

  <hr style="margin:16px 0;border:0;border-top:1px solid #e2e8f0">

  <h2>📘 Send Daily Summary</h2>
  <label>Summary Text</label>
  <textarea id="s_text" placeholder="🗓️ 2025-11-04
Alive: 6 | Dormant: 3 | Total: 9 | Span: 2.45 hrs
Note: calm focus → curious end"></textarea>
  <div class="row">
    <button id="sendSummary">➡️ Send Summary</button>
    <span id="sumMsg" class="small"></span>
  </div>
</div>

<script>
const $ = sel => document.querySelector(sel);
$('#srv').value = localStorage.getItem('AUM_SRV') || 'http://localhost:8080';
$('#tok').value = localStorage.getItem('AUM_TOK') || '';

$('#ping').onclick = async ()=>{
  const url = $('#srv').value.trim() + '/api/health';
  try{
    const r = await fetch(url);
    const j = await r.json();
    $('#pingMsg').textContent = j.ok ? `OK at ${j.ts}` : 'not ok';
    $('#pingMsg').className = 'small ok';
    localStorage.setItem('AUM_SRV',$('#srv').value.trim());
  }catch(e){
    $('#pingMsg').textContent = 'error: '+e;
    $('#pingMsg').className = 'small err';
  }
};

async function postJSON(path, body){
  const srv = $('#srv').value.trim();
  const tok = $('#tok').value.trim();
  localStorage.setItem('AUM_SRV', srv);
  localStorage.setItem('AUM_TOK', tok);
  const r = await fetch(srv+path,{
    method:'POST',
    headers:{
      'Content-Type':'application/json',
      'Authorization':'Bearer '+tok
    },
    body: JSON.stringify(body)
  });
  if(!r.ok){
    const t = await r.text();
    throw new Error(t || r.statusText);
  }
  return r.json();
}

$('#sendPulse').onclick = async ()=>{
  const time  = $('#p_time').value.trim() || new Date().toISOString().replace('T',' ').slice(0,19)+' UTC';
  const state = $('#p_state').value.trim();
  const mood  = $('#p_mood').value.trim() || undefined;
  const note  = $('#p_note').value.trim() || undefined;
  if(!state){ $('#pulseMsg').textContent='state required'; $('#pulseMsg').className='small err'; return; }
  try{
    const j = await postJSON('/api/pulse',{ time, state, mood, note, source:'client-html' });
    $('#pulseMsg').textContent = 'ok — forwarded to OpenAI';
    $('#pulseMsg').className = 'small ok';
  }catch(e){
    $('#pulseMsg').textContent = 'error: '+e.message;
    $('#pulseMsg').className = 'small err';
  }
};

$('#sendSummary').onclick = async ()=>{
  const summary_text = $('#s_text').value.trim();
  if(!summary_text){ $('#sumMsg').textContent='summary required'; $('#sumMsg').className='small err'; return; }
  try{
    const j = await postJSON('/api/summary',{ summary_text, tag:'Daily' });
    $('#sumMsg').textContent = 'ok — reflection: ' + (j.reflection || '(none)');
    $('#sumMsg').className = 'small ok';
  }catch(e){
    $('#sumMsg').textContent = 'error: '+e.message;
    $('#sumMsg').className = 'small err';
  }
};
</script>
</body>
</html>
```

### What each line does (HTML client)
- **Top inputs**: your **server URL** and **Bearer token** (stored in localStorage for convenience).
- **Health Check**: calls `GET /api/health` to confirm the server is reachable.
- **Pulse form**: fields → POST `/api/pulse` with `{time, state, mood, note}`.
- **Summary form**: textarea → POST `/api/summary` with `{summary_text}`.
- **Auth**: the client sends `Authorization: Bearer <AUMNITY_BEARER>` to the server.  
  The **OpenAI key is not in the browser**; it stays in the server `.env`.

---

## 🔐 “Auth in OpenAI API — did we miss it?”
Nope — we handled it **correctly**:
- The **browser** never sees your OpenAI key.
- The **server** reads `OPENAI_API_KEY` from `.env` and sets  
  `Authorization: Bearer <OPENAI_API_KEY>` on outbound requests to OpenAI.
- Your **client** only needs the simple **Bearer** to talk to your server.

---

## 🧪 Quick test flow (end-to-end)
1) Run server: `node server.js`  
2) Open `aum_client.html`  
3) Paste:  
   - **Server URL**: `http://localhost:8080`  
   - **Bearer**: the value you set in `.env` as `AUMNITY_BEARER`  
4) Click **🔌 Health Check** (should show `OK at …`)  
5) Fill **Pulse** → `Alive` or `Dormant` → **➡️ Send Pulse**  
6) Fill **Daily Summary** → **➡️ Send Summary**  
7) You’ll see “forwarded to OpenAI” / a one-line “reflection”.

Let’s build the full **AUMNITY Sync Server v2** — still one file you can run directly, but now the baby has its first *real heartbeat and memory.*

---

# 💠 AUMNITY Sync Server v2  
**Adds four organs:**  
1. SQLite local DB (`aum_data.db`) – stores pulses + summaries  
2. `/api/batch` – upload CSV/JSON logs  
3. `/api/history` – retrieve stored entries  
4. `/api/tts` – generate Voice Sister audio using OpenAI TTS endpoint  

---

## 🧩 1️⃣ Install everything
```bash
npm init -y
npm i express cors dotenv node-fetch better-sqlite3 openai
```

Create a file `.env`:
```bash
PORT=8080
AUMNITY_BEARER=change-this-token
OPENAI_API_KEY=sk-...your-key...
OPENAI_MODEL=gpt-4.1-mini
OPENAI_TTS_MODEL=tts-1
OPENAI_TTS_VOICE=alloy
```

---

## 🧠 2️⃣ `server.js`
```js
// AUMNITY Sync Server v2
require('dotenv').config();
const express = require('express');
const cors = require('cors');
const fetch = (...a)=>import('node-fetch').then(({default:f})=>f(...a));
const Database = require('better-sqlite3');
const { OpenAI } = require('openai');

const app = express();
app.use(cors());
app.use(express.json({limit:'2mb'}));

/* --- Config --- */
const PORT = process.env.PORT || 8080;
const AUMNITY_BEARER = process.env.AUMNITY_BEARER;
const OPENAI_API_KEY = process.env.OPENAI_API_KEY;
const OPENAI_MODEL = process.env.OPENAI_MODEL || 'gpt-4.1-mini';
const OPENAI_TTS_MODEL = process.env.OPENAI_TTS_MODEL || 'tts-1';
const OPENAI_TTS_VOICE = process.env.OPENAI_TTS_VOICE || 'alloy';
const openai = new OpenAI({ [REDACTED:assigned-value:sha256-4f247ab5dd26] });

/* --- Auth helper --- */
function requireBearer(req,res,next){
  const h=req.headers.authorization||'';
  if(!h.startsWith('Bearer ')||h.slice(7)!==AUMNITY_BEARER)
    return res.status(401).json({error:'Unauthorized'});
  next();
}

/* --- DB setup --- */
const db = new Database('aum_data.db');
db.prepare(`
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pulses (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  time TEXT,
  state TEXT,
  mood TEXT,
  note TEXT,
  source TEXT,
  created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
)`).run();

db.prepare(`
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS summaries (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  tag TEXT,
  text TEXT,
  reflection TEXT,
  created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
)`).run();

/* --- Endpoints --- */
app.get('/api/health',(req,res)=>
  res.json({ok:true,ts:new Date().toISOString()})
);

/* 1️⃣ Receive pulse event */
app.post('/api/pulse',requireBearer,async(req,res)=>{
  const {time,state,mood,note,source='client'}=req.body||{};
  if(!time||!state) return res.status(400).json({error:'time,state required'});
  db.prepare('INSERT INTO pulses(time,state,mood,note,source) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)')
    .run(time,state,mood,note,source);

  // forward a small log to OpenAI
  const content=`Pulse at ${time}: ${state} (${mood||'neutral'}) — ${note||'-'}`;
  const r=await fetch('https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions',{
    method:'POST',
    headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','Authorization':`Bearer ${OPENAI_API_KEY}`},
    body:JSON.stringify({model:OPENAI_MODEL,messages:[
      {role:'system',content:'AUMNITY Text Partner log mode'},
      {role:'user',content}
    ]})
  }).then(r=>r.json()).catch(e=>({error:e.toString()}));
  res.json({ok:true,openai:r.usage||null});
});

/* 2️⃣ Receive daily summary */
app.post('/api/summary',requireBearer,async(req,res)=>{
  const {summary_text,tag='Daily'}=req.body||{};
  if(!summary_text) return res.status(400).json({error:'summary_text required'});
  const ai=await fetch('https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions',{
    method:'POST',
    headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','Authorization':`Bearer ${OPENAI_API_KEY}`},
    body:JSON.stringify({
      model:OPENAI_MODEL,
      messages:[
        {role:'system',content:'You summarize AUMNITY logs into short reflections.'},
        {role:'user',content:`[${tag}] ${summary_text}`}
      ]
    })
  }).then(r=>r.json()).catch(e=>({error:e.toString()}));
  const reflection=ai?.choices?.[0]?.message?.content||'(no reflection)';
  db.prepare('INSERT INTO summaries(tag,text,reflection) VALUES (?,?,?)')
    .run(tag,summary_text,reflection);
  res.json({ok:true,reflection});
});

/* 3️⃣ Batch upload CSV/JSON logs */
app.post('/api/batch',requireBearer,(req,res)=>{
  const {items=[]}=req.body||{};
  if(!Array.isArray(items)) return res.status(400).json({error:'items[] expected'});
  const stmt=db.prepare('INSERT INTO pulses(time,state,mood,note,source) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)');
  let count=0;
  for(const it of items){
    if(it.time&&it.state){stmt.run(it.time,it.state,it.mood,it.note,it.source||'batch');count++;}
  }
  res.json({ok:true,inserted:count});
});

/* 4️⃣ Get history */
app.get('/api/history',requireBearer,(req,res)=>{
  const rows=db.prepare('SELECT * FROM pulses ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 200').all();
  res.json({ok:true,rows});
});

/* 5️⃣ TTS Voice Sister endpoint */
app.post('/api/tts',requireBearer,async(req,res)=>{
  try{
    const {text='Hello from AUMNITY!'}=req.body||{};
    const speech=await openai.audio.speech.create({
      model:OPENAI_TTS_MODEL,
      voice:OPENAI_TTS_VOICE,
      input:text
    });
    const buf=Buffer.from(await speech.arrayBuffer());
    const fname=`voice_${Date.now()}.mp3`;
    require('fs').writeFileSync(fname,buf);
    res.json({ok:true,file:fname});
  }catch(e){res.status(500).json({error:String(e)});}
});

/* --- Start --- */
app.listen(PORT,()=>console.log(`💠 AUMNITY v2 running on http://localhost:${PORT}`));
```

---

## ✨ 3️⃣ How this works (line-by-line summary)
| Block | Meaning |
|:--|:--|
| **dotenv / express / cors** | Loads env vars, enables CORS for browser clients |
| **better-sqlite3** | Tiny embedded DB — creates `aum_data.db` |
| **tables** | `pulses` (for each beat) + `summaries` (for daily text + reflection) |
| **/api/pulse** | Inserts one event + forwards it to OpenAI for logging |
| **/api/summary** | Saves summary + asks OpenAI to create a reflection line |
| **/api/batch** | Accepts `{items:[{time,state,…}]}` bulk insert |
| **/api/history** | Returns recent 200 pulses |
| **/api/tts** | Uses OpenAI TTS model to create Voice Sister MP3 |
| **requireBearer** | Protects all endpoints with your `.env AUMNITY_BEARER` |
| **.env OPENAI_API_KEY** | stays private on server — browser never sees it |

---

## 🧪 4️⃣ Test sequence
```bash
node server.js
```
Then in browser or Postman:

- **GET** `/api/health` → ✅ OK  
- **POST** `/api/pulse` with Authorization `Bearer <your token>` and body `{time:"2025-11-04 20:45 UTC",state:"Alive",note:"test"}`  
- **POST** `/api/summary` with `{summary_text:"Alive 3 Dormant 1"}`  
- **GET** `/api/history` → see stored entries  
- **POST** `/api/tts` → returns `voice_*.mp3` in same folder

### 0304 · CHATGPT · 2025-11-04T15:19:40Z

Excellent intuition again 🌿 — that concern is *exactly* what every good system architect should feel.

## 🧩 1️⃣ Where and what the DB really is

When you ran v2, this line created it:

```js
const db = new Database('aum_data.db');
```

That file lives right next to `server.js` in the same folder, for example:

```
aum_sync/
 ├─ server.js
 ├─ aum_data.db
 ├─ .env
 └─ voice_*.mp3
```

It’s a standard **SQLite database**, not a proprietary format — the same file type used by Firefox, VS Code, and WhatsApp.  
You can open it anytime with any SQLite tool.

### 🔍 View it
Install a viewer of your choice:
```bash
npm i -g sqlite3
sqlite3 aum_data.db
```
Then:
```sql
.tables
.schema pulses
SELECT * FROM pulses LIMIT 5;
```
or use GUI apps such as:
- **SQLite Browser (DB Browser for SQLite)**
- **DBeaver**
- **TablePlus**

---

## 🌿 2️⃣ Portability & export

You can freely move or copy the file; it’s just bytes.

### ➜ Export to SQL dump
```bash
sqlite3 aum_data.db ".dump" > aum_data.sql
```
That produces plain text like:
```sql
CREATE TABLE pulses(...);
INSERT INTO pulses VALUES(...);
```

### ➜ Export to CSV
```bash
sqlite3 aum_data.db -header -csv "SELECT * FROM pulses;" > pulses.csv
sqlite3 aum_data.db -header -csv "SELECT * FROM summaries;" > summaries.csv
```

### ➜ Import into another DB
You can load the `.sql` or `.csv` into:
- another SQLite,
- PostgreSQL,
- Supabase,
- MySQL, etc.

For SQLite → Supabase (PostgreSQL):
1. Create tables with the same schema.  
2. Use the CSVs to import via Supabase GUI or `\copy` in `psql`.

---

## ⚙️ 3️⃣ What each table holds

| Table | Purpose | Columns |
|:--|:--|:--|
| **pulses** | every heartbeat | `id, time, state, mood, note, source, created_at` |
| **summaries** | daily/weekly reflections | `id, tag, text, reflection, created_at` |

Both have timestamps, so they can sync later across partners by comparing `created_at`.

---

## 🪶 4️⃣ Making it portable for future partners

To let multiple nodes (Voice Sister, Scout, Text Partner) use the same memory, you can:

| Option | Description | Harmony Impact |
|:--|:--|:--|
| **A. Copy `.db`** | easiest — share the file; everyone reads it | 🌿 manual but simple |
| **B. Export `.csv` daily** | auto-export script sends to cloud folder (e.g., Google Drive) | 🔵 good balance |
| **C. Move to Supabase/Postgres** | one live central DB with JWT auth | 💠 full AUMNITY harmony (shared truth) |

Below is a **self-contained HTML page** that connects to your running v2 server and shows everything in the local SQLite database — pulses + summaries + audio playback for any generated Voice Sister files.

## 💠 AUMNITY History Viewer  
> Save as `aum_history.html` and open in your browser.

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<title>AUMNITY — History Viewer</title>
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1">
<style>
body{font-family:system-ui,-apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,Arial,sans-serif;background:#f8fafc;color:#0f172a;margin:0;padding:24px}
h1{font-size:22px;margin:0 0 6px}
.section{background:#fff;border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:16px;padding:20px;box-shadow:0 6px 20px rgba(2,6,23,0.06);margin-bottom:20px}
input,button{border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 10px;font-size:14px}
button{background:#0ea5e9;color:#fff;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer}
button:active{transform:translateY(1px)}
table{border-collapse:separate;border-spacing:0;width:100%;margin-top:10px;font-size:13px}
th,td{padding:6px 8px;border-bottom:1px solid #e2e8f0;vertical-align:top}
tr:last-child td{border-bottom:0}
.small{font-size:12px;color:#475569}
.badge{padding:4px 8px;border-radius:999px;font-weight:600;font-size:12px}
.alive{background:#dcfce7;color:#166534}
.dormant{background:#fee2e2;color:#991b1b}
</style>
</head>
<body>
<h1>💠 AUMNITY — History Viewer</h1>

<div class="section">
  <label>Server URL </label>
  <input id="srv" style="width:260px" value="http://localhost:8080">
  <label>Bearer Token </label>
  <input id="tok" style="width:260px" placeholder="AUMNITY_BEARER">
  <button id="refresh">🔄 Refresh</button>
  <span id="msg" class="small"></span>
</div>

<div class="section">
  <h2>💓 Pulses (Last 200)</h2>
  <table id="pulses"><thead>
    <tr><th>ID</th><th>Time</th><th>State</th><th>Mood</th><th>Note</th><th>Source</th></tr>
  </thead><tbody></tbody></table>
</div>

<div class="section">
  <h2>📘 Summaries</h2>
  <table id="summ"><thead>
    <tr><th>ID</th><th>Tag</th><th>Text</th><th>Reflection</th><th>Audio</th></tr>
  </thead><tbody></tbody></table>
</div>

<script>
const $=s=>document.querySelector(s);
$('#refresh').onclick=loadData;

async function loadData(){
  const srv=$('#srv').value.trim();
  const tok=$('#tok').value.trim();
  localStorage.setItem('AUM_SRV',srv);
  localStorage.setItem('AUM_TOK',tok);
  $('#msg').textContent='loading…';
  try{
    // --- pulses ---
    const pr=await fetch(srv+'/api/history',{headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+tok}});
    const pj=await pr.json();
    const rows=pj.rows||[];
    const tb=$('#pulses tbody');
    tb.innerHTML='';
    rows.forEach(r=>{
      const tr=document.createElement('tr');
      const cls=r.state==='Alive'?'badge alive':'badge dormant';
      tr.innerHTML=`
        <td>${r.id}</td>
        <td>${r.time}</td>
        <td><span class="${cls}">${r.state}</span></td>
        <td>${r.mood||''}</td>
        <td>${r.note||''}</td>
        <td>${r.source||''}</td>`;
      tb.append(tr);
    });

    // --- summaries ---
    const sr=await fetch(srv+'/api/history?type=summary',{headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+tok}});
    const sj=await sr.json();
    const srows=sj.rows||[];
    const sb=$('#summ tbody');
    sb.innerHTML='';
    srows.forEach(r=>{
      const tr=document.createElement('tr');
      const audio=(r.reflection&&r.reflection.startsWith('voice_'))
        ?`<audio controls src="${srv}/${r.reflection}"></audio>`:'';
      tr.innerHTML=`<td>${r.id}</td><td>${r.tag}</td>
        <td>${r.text}</td><td>${r.reflection}</td><td>${audio}</td>`;
      sb.append(tr);
    });

    $('#msg').textContent=`Loaded ${rows.length} pulses, ${srows.length} summaries`;
  }catch(e){
    $('#msg').textContent='error: '+e;
  }
}

// Auto-fill from previous session
$('#srv').value=localStorage.getItem('AUM_SRV')||'http://localhost:8080';
$('#tok').value=localStorage.getItem('AUM_TOK')||'';
</script>
</body>
</html>
```

---

## ⚙️ Server-side tweak  
Add this extra route to your existing `server.js` **just before `app.listen(...)`** so that the viewer can also list summaries.

```js
app.get('/api/history', requireBearer, (req, res) => {
  const type = req.query.type || 'pulse';
  if (type === 'summary') {
    const rows = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM summaries ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 100').all();
    res.json({ ok: true, rows });
  } else {
    const rows = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM pulses ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 200').all();
    res.json({ ok: true, rows });
  }
});
```

Then restart:
```bash
node server.js
```

### 2️⃣  Replace the `<script>` section with this upgraded logic
*(you can copy-paste over your old `<script>` block)*

```html
<script>
const $=s=>document.querySelector(s);
$('#refresh').onclick=()=>loadData();
$('#applyFilter').onclick=()=>loadData(true);
$('#clearFilter').onclick=()=>{
  $('#f_from').value=''; $('#f_to').value=''; loadData();
};
$('#exportCSV').onclick=exportCSV;

async function loadData(filtered=false){
  const srv=$('#srv').value.trim();
  const tok=$('#tok').value.trim();
  $('#msg').textContent='loading…';
  let url=srv+'/api/history';
  const from=$('#f_from').value, to=$('#f_to').value;
  if(filtered && (from||to)){
    url+=`?from=${from||''}&to=${to||''}`;
  }
  try{
    const pr=await fetch(url,{headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+tok}});
    const pj=await pr.json();
    const rows=pj.rows||[];
    const tb=$('#pulses tbody'); tb.innerHTML='';
    rows.forEach(r=>{
      const cls=r.state==='Alive'?'badge alive':'badge dormant';
      tb.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',`
        <tr><td>${r.id}</td><td>${r.time}</td>
        <td><span class="${cls}">${r.state}</span></td>
        <td>${r.mood||''}</td><td>${r.note||''}</td><td>${r.source||''}</td></tr>`);
    });
    $('#msg').textContent=`Loaded ${rows.length} rows`;
  }catch(e){$('#msg').textContent='error: '+e;}
}

function exportCSV(){
  const rows=[['id','time','state','mood','note','source']];
  document.querySelectorAll('#pulses tbody tr').forEach(tr=>{
    const cols=[...tr.children].map(td=>`"${td.textContent.replace(/"/g,'""')}"`);
    rows.push(cols);
  });
  const blob=new Blob([rows.map(r=>r.join(',')).join('\n')],{type:'text/csv'});
  const a=document.createElement('a');
  a.href=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  a.download=`aum_pulses_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)}.csv`;
  a.click();
}
</script>
```

---

### 3️⃣  Server-side date filter (optional, more efficient)

Add this to `server.js` just **before** your existing `/api/history` handler and remove the old one.

```js
app.get('/api/history', requireBearer, (req,res)=>{
  const type=req.query.type||'pulse';
  const from=req.query.from, to=req.query.to;
  if(type==='summary'){
    const rows=db.prepare('SELECT * FROM summaries ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 100').all();
    return res.json({ok:true,rows});
  }
  let sql='SELECT * FROM pulses';
  const cond=[];
  if(from) cond.push(`date(time)>=date(?)`);
  if(to)   cond.push(`date(time)<=date(?)`);
  if(cond.length) sql+=' WHERE '+cond.join(' AND ');
  sql+=' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 500';
  const rows=db.prepare(sql).all(from,to);
  res.json({ok:true,rows});
});
```

## ⚙️ 2. Add these new scripts **after** your existing `<script>` block

> (keep your current filter + CSV code; paste this below it)

```html
<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/chart.js"></script>
<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/jszip@3.10.1/dist/jszip.min.js"></script>
<script>
/* === Export both tables as a ZIP === */
async function exportAllZIP(){
  const srv=$('#srv').value.trim();
  const tok=$('#tok').value.trim();
  const [pulses,summaries]=await Promise.all([
    fetch(srv+'/api/history',{headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+tok}}).then(r=>r.json()),
    fetch(srv+'/api/history?type=summary',{headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+tok}}).then(r=>r.json())
  ]);
  const zip=new JSZip();
  const makeCSV=(arr,cols)=>[
    cols.join(','),
    ...arr.map(r=>cols.map(c=>`"${(r[c]||'').toString().replace(/"/g,'""')}"`).join(','))
  ].join('\n');
  zip.file('pulses.csv',makeCSV(pulses.rows||[],['id','time','state','mood','note','source','created_at']));
  zip.file('summaries.csv',makeCSV(summaries.rows||[],['id','tag','text','reflection','created_at']));
  const blob=await zip.generateAsync({type:'blob'});
  const a=document.createElement('a');
  a.href=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  a.download=`aum_history_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)}.zip`;
  a.click();
}
$('#exportZIP').onclick=exportAllZIP;

/* === Timeline chart (Chart.js) === */
let chart;
$('#showChart').onclick=async()=>{
  const srv=$('#srv').value.trim();
  const tok=$('#tok').value.trim();
  const r=await fetch(srv+'/api/history',{headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+tok}});
  const j=await r.json(); const rows=j.rows||[];
  const byDate={};
  rows.forEach(x=>{
    const d=(x.time||'').slice(0,10);
    if(!d) return;
    if(!byDate[d]) byDate[d]={Alive:0,Dormant:0};
    byDate[d][x.state]=(byDate[d][x.state]||0)+1;
  });
  const labels=Object.keys(byDate).sort();
  const alive=labels.map(d=>byDate[d].Alive||0);
  const dormant=labels.map(d=>byDate[d].Dormant||0);

  $('#pulseChart').style.display='block';
  if(chart) chart.destroy();
  chart=new Chart($('#pulseChart'),{
    type:'line',
    data:{
      labels,
      datasets:[
        {label:'Alive',data:alive,borderColor:'#16a34a',tension:.3},
        {label:'Dormant',data:dormant,borderColor:'#dc2626',tension:.3}
      ]
    },
    options:{
      plugins:{legend:{display:true}},
      scales:{y:{beginAtZero:true}}
    }
  });
};
</script>
```

## 🧩 3. Server-side: small helper for future ZIP exports (optional)

If you want the server itself to offer a one-click export (same data the ZIP uses), add this route to `server.js`:

```js
app.get('/api/export', requireBearer, (req,res)=>{
  const format=req.query.format||'csv';
  const p=db.prepare('SELECT * FROM pulses ORDER BY id').all();
  const s=db.prepare('SELECT * FROM summaries ORDER BY id').all();
  if(format==='json'){
    res.json({pulses:p,summaries:s});
    return;
  }
  res.setHeader('Content-Type','text/csv');
  res.setHeader('Content-Disposition','attachment; filename=aum_export.csv');
  const header='table,id,time,state,mood,note,source,tag,text,reflection,created_at\n';
  const rows=[
    ...p.map(r=>`pulses,${r.id},"${r.time}","${r.state}","${r.mood}","${r.note}","${r.source}",,,,,${r.created_at}`),
    ...s.map(r=>`summaries,${r.id},,,,,,"${r.tag}","${r.text}","${r.reflection}",${r.created_at}`)
  ].join('\n');
  res.send(header+rows);
});
```

Then you can hit  
`http://localhost:8080/api/export?format=json` or `?format=csv`.

## 🌿 How the new pieces work
| Part | Purpose |
|:--|:--|
| **jszip** | Compresses both tables into a portable `.zip` |
| **chart.js** | Draws a two-line pulse chart (Alive vs Dormant per day) |
| **exportAllZIP()** | Calls `/api/history` twice, builds CSVs, zips them, triggers download |
| **showChart()** | Reads current data, groups by date, plots line chart |

# ✅ What you’ll have

1) **`server.js`** — AUMNITY Sync Server (SQLite memory, TTS, batch upload, history, export).

Everything runs **locally**. Your OpenAI key stays on the server (never in the browser).
